namespace AspenTech.EquipmentAnalysis.Engine;

public struct ApiOrificeConstraints
{
	public readonly int MinT;

	public readonly int MaxT;

	public readonly int MaxSetP;

	public readonly int OutletPLimit;

	public readonly Temperature MinTemperature;

	public readonly Temperature MaxTemperature;

	public readonly Pressure MaxSetPressure;

	public readonly Pressure OutletPressureLimit;

	public readonly bool IsSetPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit;

	public readonly bool IsOutletPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit;

	public ApiOrificeConstraints(int minTemperature, int maxTemperature, int maxSetP, int outletPressureLimit, bool isSetPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit = true, bool isOutletPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit = true)
	{
		MinT = minTemperature;
		MaxT = maxTemperature;
		MaxSetP = maxSetP;
		OutletPLimit = outletPressureLimit;
		MinTemperature = ApiTables.Quantities.GetInF(MyMath.RoundedInt(MinT, 5));
		MaxTemperature = ApiTables.Quantities.GetInF(MyMath.RoundedInt(MaxT, 5));
		MaxSetPressure = ApiTables.Quantities.GetInPsig(maxSetP);
		OutletPressureLimit = ApiTables.Quantities.GetInPsig(OutletPLimit);
		IsSetPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit = isSetPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit;
		IsOutletPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit = isOutletPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit;
	}

	public static bool operator ==(ApiOrificeConstraints lhs, ApiOrificeConstraints rhs)
	{
		if (lhs.MinT == rhs.MinT && lhs.MaxT == rhs.MaxT && lhs.MaxSetP == rhs.MaxSetP)
		{
			return lhs.OutletPLimit == rhs.OutletPLimit;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool operator !=(ApiOrificeConstraints lhs, ApiOrificeConstraints rhs)
	{
		return !(lhs == rhs);
	}

	public bool Equals(ApiOrificeConstraints other)
	{
		if (MinT == other.MinT && MaxT == other.MaxT && MaxSetP == other.MaxSetP)
		{
			return OutletPLimit == other.OutletPLimit;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public override bool Equals(object obj)
	{
		if (obj == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (obj is ApiOrificeConstraints)
		{
			return Equals((ApiOrificeConstraints)obj);
		}
		return false;
	}

	public override int GetHashCode()
	{
		return (((((MinT * 397) ^ MaxT) * 397) ^ MaxSetP) * 397) ^ OutletPLimit;
	}
}
public abstract class ApiTables
{
	internal class NodeA3Values : Dictionary<TemperatureRange, Tuple<int, bool>[]>
	{
	}

	internal static class Node3Builder
	{
		internal static Node3 Create(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, NodeA3Values setPressureLimits, IReadOnlyList<Tuple<int, bool>> backpressureLimits)
		{
			return new Node3(CreateA3(vafs, setPressureLimits), CreateB3(vafs, backpressureLimits));
		}

		internal static NodeA3 CreateA3(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, NodeA3Values setPressureLimits)
		{
			NodeA3 nodeA = new NodeA3();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<TemperatureRange, Tuple<int, bool>[]> setPressureLimit in setPressureLimits)
			{
				nodeA.Add(setPressureLimit.Key, CreateA4(vafs, setPressureLimit.Value));
			}
			return nodeA;
		}

		private static NodeA4 CreateA4(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, IReadOnlyList<Tuple<int, bool>> values)
		{
			NodeA4 nodeA = new NodeA4();
			AddValues(vafs, values, nodeA);
			return nodeA;
		}

		internal static NodeB3 CreateB3(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, IReadOnlyList<Tuple<int, bool>> values)
		{
			NodeB3 nodeB = new NodeB3();
			AddValues(vafs, values, nodeB);
			return nodeB;
		}

		private static void AddValues(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, IReadOnlyList<Tuple<int, bool>> values, IDictionary<ValveAndFlange, Tuple<int, bool>> node)
		{
			int num = Math.Min(vafs.Count, values.Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				node.Add(vafs[i], values[i]);
			}
		}
	}

	protected class PilotOperatedBuilder
	{
		private readonly IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> _vafs;

		private readonly IReadOnlyList<int> _setIndices;

		private readonly IReadOnlyList<int> _backIndices;

		private readonly bool _isNiCuVafListRestricted;

		private PilotOperatedBuilder(IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, IReadOnlyList<int> setIndices, IReadOnlyList<int> backIndices, bool isNiCuVafListRestricted)
		{
			_vafs = vafs;
			_setIndices = setIndices;
			_backIndices = backIndices;
			_isNiCuVafListRestricted = isNiCuVafListRestricted;
		}

		private Node2 Create(ApiTables parent)
		{
			return new Node2
			{
				{
					ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel,
					Node3Builder.Create(_vafs, ExtractValues(parent.GetCarbon4Values(), _setIndices, isNiCu: false), _ConvertIntArray(ExtractValues(parent.GetCarbonB3Values(), _backIndices, isNiCu: false)))
				},
				{
					ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless,
					Node3Builder.Create(_vafs, ExtractValues(parent.GetAustenitic4Values(), _setIndices, isNiCu: false), _ConvertIntArray(ExtractValues(parent.GetAusteniticB3Values(), _backIndices, isNiCu: false)))
				},
				{
					ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper,
					Node3Builder.Create(_GetNiCuVafList(), ExtractValues(parent.GetNiCu4Values(), _setIndices, isNiCu: true), _ConvertIntArray(ExtractValues(parent.GetNonSteelB3Values(), _backIndices, isNiCu: true)))
				},
				{
					ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20,
					Node3Builder.Create(_vafs, ExtractValues(parent.GetAlloy204Values(), _setIndices, isNiCu: false), _ConvertIntArray(ExtractValues(parent.GetNonSteelB3Values(), _backIndices, isNiCu: false)))
				}
			};
		}

		private IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> _GetNiCuVafList()
		{
			if (!_isNiCuVafListRestricted)
			{
				return _vafs;
			}
			List<ValveAndFlange> list = new List<ValveAndFlange>();
			foreach (ValveAndFlange vaf in _vafs)
			{
				if (vaf.InletFlange <= 900)
				{
					list.Add(vaf);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private int[] ExtractValues(IReadOnlyList<int> values, IReadOnlyList<int> indices, bool isNiCu)
		{
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			for (int i = 0; i < indices.Count; i++)
			{
				ValveAndFlange valveAndFlange = _vafs[i];
				if (!isNiCu || !_isNiCuVafListRestricted || valveAndFlange.InletFlange <= 900)
				{
					int num = indices[i];
					if (num < values.Count)
					{
						list.Add(values[num]);
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		private Tuple<int, bool>[] ExtractValues(IReadOnlyList<Tuple<int, bool>> values, IReadOnlyList<int> indices, bool isNiCu)
		{
			List<Tuple<int, bool>> list = new List<Tuple<int, bool>>();
			for (int i = 0; i < indices.Count; i++)
			{
				ValveAndFlange valveAndFlange = _vafs[i];
				if (!isNiCu || !_isNiCuVafListRestricted || valveAndFlange.InletFlange <= 900)
				{
					int num = indices[i];
					if (num < values.Count)
					{
						list.Add(new Tuple<int, bool>(values[num].Item1, item2: true));
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		private NodeA3Values ExtractValues(NodeA3Values values, IReadOnlyList<int> indices, bool isNiCu)
		{
			NodeA3Values nodeA3Values = new NodeA3Values();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<TemperatureRange, Tuple<int, bool>[]> value in values)
			{
				nodeA3Values.Add(value.Key, ExtractValues(value.Value, indices, isNiCu));
			}
			return nodeA3Values;
		}

		internal static Node2 Create(ApiTables parent, IReadOnlyList<ValveAndFlange> vafs, IReadOnlyList<int> setIndices, IReadOnlyList<int> backIndices, bool isNiCuVafListRestricted)
		{
			return new PilotOperatedBuilder(vafs, setIndices, backIndices, isNiCuVafListRestricted).Create(parent);
		}

		protected Tuple<int, bool>[] _ConvertIntArray(int[] array)
		{
			Tuple<int, bool>[] array2 = new Tuple<int, bool>[array.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array2[i] = Tuple.Create(array[i], item2: true);
			}
			return array2;
		}
	}

		protected int ChromeMolyMinTemperature => 451;

		protected abstract NodeA3Values SlAusteniticSetPressureLimits { get; }

		protected abstract int[] BellowsAusteniticBackpressureLimits { get; }

		protected abstract NodeA3Values SlNiCuSetPressureLimits { get; }

		protected abstract ValveAndFlange[] SlAlloy20Vafs { get; }

		protected Node3 ConventionalChromeMoly => Node3Builder.Create(SlChromeMolyVafs, SlChromeMolySetPressureLimits, ConventionalChromeMolyBackpressureLimits);

		protected Node3 ConventionalAustenitic => Node3Builder.Create(SlAusteniticVafs, SlAusteniticSetPressureLimits, ConventionalAusteniticBackpressureLimits);

		protected Node3 ConventionalNiCu => Node3Builder.Create(SlNiCuVafs, SlNiCuSetPressureLimits, ConventionalNiCuBackpressureLimits);

		protected Node3 ConventionalAlloy20 => Node3Builder.Create(SlAlloy20Vafs, SlAlloy20SetPressureLimits, ConventionalAlloy20BackpressureLimits);

		protected Node3 BellowsCarbonSteel => Node3Builder.Create(SlCarbonSteelVafs, SlCarbonSteelSetPressureLimits, _ConvertIntArray(BellowsCarbonSteelBackpressureLimits));

		protected Node3 BellowsChromeMoly => Node3Builder.Create(SlChromeMolyVafs, SlChromeMolySetPressureLimits, _ConvertIntArray(BellowsChromeMolyBackpressureLimits));

		protected Node3 BellowsAustenitic => Node3Builder.Create(SlAusteniticVafs, SlAusteniticSetPressureLimits, _ConvertIntArray(BellowsAusteniticBackpressureLimits));

		protected Node3 BellowsNiCu => Node3Builder.Create(SlNiCuVafs, SlNiCuSetPressureLimits, _ConvertIntArray(BellowsNiCuBackpressureLimits));

		protected Node3 BellowsAlloy20 => Node3Builder.Create(SlAlloy20Vafs, SlAlloy20SetPressureLimits, _ConvertIntArray(BellowsAlloy20BackpressureLimits));

		protected Node2 Conventional => new Node2
		{
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel,
				ConventionalCarbonSteel
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.ChromeMoly,
				ConventionalChromeMoly
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless,
				ConventionalAustenitic
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper,
				ConventionalNiCu
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20,
				ConventionalAlloy20
			}
		};

		protected Node2 Bellows => new Node2
		{
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel,
				BellowsCarbonSteel
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.ChromeMoly,
				BellowsChromeMoly
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless,
				BellowsAustenitic
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper,
				BellowsNiCu
			},
			{
				ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20,
				BellowsAlloy20
			}
		};

		protected Node2 PilotOperatedEncapsulated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_pilotOperatedEncapsulated == null)
				{
					_pilotOperatedEncapsulated = PilotOperatedBuilder.Create(_parent, PoValveAndFlanges, PoSetIndices, PoBackIndices, _isNiCuPilotVafListRestricted);
				}
				return _pilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal virtual Node2 PilotOperated => PilotOperatedEncapsulated;

		internal virtual Node1 Data => new Node1
		{
			{
				ValveTypeEnum.Conventional,
				Conventional
			},
			{

				Bellows
			},
			{
				ValveTypeEnum.PilotOperated,
				PilotOperated
			}
		};

		protected Node1Builder(ApiTables parent, bool isNiCuPilotVafListRestricted = false)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_isNiCuPilotVafListRestricted = isNiCuPilotVafListRestricted;
		}

		protected Tuple<int, bool>[] _ConvertIntArray(int[] array)

			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array2[i] = Tuple.Create(array[i], item2: true);
			}
			return array2;
		}

	internal class Node0 : Dictionary<ApiDesignation, Node1>
	{
		internal IEnumerable<ApiDesignation> RelevantKeys(ApiDesignation? designation)
		{
			if (designation.HasValue)
			{
				return new ApiDesignation[1] { designation.Value };
			}
			return base.Keys;
		}
	}

			{
				return new ValveTypeEnum[1] { psvType.Value };
			}
			return base.Keys;
		}
	}

	internal class Node2 : Dictionary<ApiValveMaterial, Node3>
	{
		internal IEnumerable<ApiValveMaterial> RelevantKeys(ApiValveMaterial? valveMaterial)
		{
			if (valveMaterial.HasValue)
			{
				return new ApiValveMaterial[1] { valveMaterial.Value };
			}
			return base.Keys;
		}
	}

	internal class Node3
	{
		internal readonly NodeA3 A3;

		internal readonly NodeB3 B3;

		internal Node3(NodeA3 a3, NodeB3 b3)
		{
			A3 = a3;
			B3 = b3;
		}

		internal void Remove(ValveAndFlange vaf)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<TemperatureRange, NodeA4> item in A3)
			{
				item.Value.Remove(vaf);
			}
			B3.Remove(vaf);
		}
	}

	internal struct TemperatureRange
	{
		internal readonly int Min;

		internal readonly int Max;

		private readonly Temperature _min;

		private readonly Temperature _max;

		internal TemperatureRange(int min, int max)
		{
			Min = min;
			_min = Quantities.GetInF((double)min - 0.5);
			Max = max;
			_max = Quantities.GetInF((double)max + 0.5);
		}

		internal bool IsInRange(double t)
		{
			return IsInRange(new Temperature(t, Units.F));
		}

		internal bool IsInRange(Temperature t)
		{
			if (t > _min)
			{
				return t <= _max;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return (17 * Min + 23 * Max) % 65536;
		}
	}

	internal class NodeA3 : Dictionary<TemperatureRange, NodeA4>
	{
		internal IEnumerable<TemperatureRange> RelevantKeys(Temperature reliefTemperature)
		{
			if (!(reliefTemperature == null))
			{
				return from p in this
					where p.Key.IsInRange(reliefTemperature)
					select p.Key;
			}
			return base.Keys;
		}
	}

	internal class NodeA4 : Dictionary<ValveAndFlange, Tuple<int, bool>>
	{
		internal IEnumerable<ValveAndFlange> RelevantKeys(Pressure setPressure)
		{
			if (!(setPressure == null))
			{
				return from p in this
					where Quantities.GetInPsig(p.Value.Item1) >= setPressure
					select p.Key;
			}
			return base.Keys;
		}
	}

	internal struct ValveAndFlange
	{
		public readonly RationalNumber InletSize;

		public readonly RationalNumber OutletSize;

		public readonly int InletFlange;

		public readonly int OutletFlange;

		internal readonly RationalNumber InletCenterToFace;

		internal readonly RationalNumber OutletCenterToFace;

		public readonly bool IsLightWeight;

		internal ValveAndFlange(RationalNumber inletSize, RationalNumber outletSize, int inletFlange, int outletFlange, RationalNumber inletCenterToFace, RationalNumber outletCenterToFace, bool isLightWeight = false)
		{
			InletSize = inletSize;
			OutletSize = outletSize;
			InletFlange = inletFlange;
			OutletFlange = outletFlange;
			InletCenterToFace = inletCenterToFace;
			OutletCenterToFace = outletCenterToFace;
			IsLightWeight = isLightWeight;
		}

		internal ValveAndFlange(ValveAndFlange other, RationalNumber? inletSize = null, RationalNumber? outletSize = null, int? inletFlange = null, int? outletFlange = null, RationalNumber? inletCenterToFace = null, RationalNumber? outletCenterToFace = null, bool? isLightWeight = null)
		{
			InletSize = inletSize ?? other.InletSize;
			OutletSize = outletSize ?? other.OutletSize;
			InletFlange = inletFlange ?? other.InletFlange;
			OutletFlange = outletFlange ?? other.OutletFlange;
			InletCenterToFace = inletCenterToFace ?? other.InletCenterToFace;
			OutletCenterToFace = outletCenterToFace ?? other.OutletCenterToFace;
			IsLightWeight = isLightWeight ?? other.IsLightWeight;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return InletSize.GetHashCode() * 17 + OutletSize.GetHashCode() * 19 + InletFlange.GetHashCode() * 41 + OutletFlange.GetHashCode() * 43 + InletCenterToFace.GetHashCode() * 59 + OutletCenterToFace.GetHashCode() * 61 + IsLightWeight.GetHashCode() * 79;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is ValveAndFlange valveAndFlange))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (InletSize == valveAndFlange.InletSize && OutletSize == valveAndFlange.OutletSize && InletFlange == valveAndFlange.InletFlange && OutletFlange == valveAndFlange.OutletFlange && InletCenterToFace == valveAndFlange.InletCenterToFace)
			{
				return OutletCenterToFace == valveAndFlange.OutletCenterToFace;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(ValveAndFlange lhs, ValveAndFlange rhs)
		{
			return lhs.Equals(rhs);
		}

		public static bool operator !=(ValveAndFlange lhs, ValveAndFlange rhs)
		{
			return !(lhs == rhs);
		}

		internal bool IsNotExcluded(RationalNumber? inletSize, RationalNumber? outletSize, int? inletFlange, int? outletFlange, bool? allowLightweight)
		{
			if ((!inletSize.HasValue || inletSize.Value == InletSize) && (!outletSize.HasValue || outletSize.Value == OutletSize) && (!inletFlange.HasValue || inletFlange.Value == InletFlange) && (!outletFlange.HasValue || outletFlange.Value == OutletFlange))
			{
				if (!(allowLightweight ?? false))
				{
					return !IsLightWeight;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return InletSize.ToMixedNumberString() + " x " + OutletSize.ToMixedNumberString() + " / " + InletFlange + (IsLightWeight ? "*" : "") + " x " + OutletFlange;
		}
	}

	internal class NodeB3 : Dictionary<ValveAndFlange, Tuple<int, bool>>
	{
		internal IEnumerable<ValveAndFlange> RelevantKeys(Pressure backPressure)
		{
			if (!(backPressure != null))
			{
				return base.Keys;
			}
			return from p in this
				where backPressure == null || backPressure <= Quantities.GetInPsig(p.Value.Item1)
				select p.Key;
		}
	}

	internal static class Quantities
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<int, Pressure> Pressures = new Dictionary<int, Pressure>();

		private static readonly Dictionary<int, Temperature> Temperatures = new Dictionary<int, Temperature>();

		private static readonly Dictionary<double, Temperature> DoubleTemperatures = new Dictionary<double, Temperature>();

		internal static Pressure GetInPsig(int p)
		{
			if (Pressures.TryGetValue(p, out var value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = Pressure.Gauge(p, Units.psi);
			Pressures.Add(p, value);
			return value;
		}

		internal static Temperature GetInF(int t)
		{
			if (Temperatures.TryGetValue(t, out var value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = new Temperature(t, Units.F);
			Temperatures.Add(t, value);
			return value;
		}

		internal static Temperature GetInF(double t)
		{
			if (DoubleTemperatures.TryGetValue(t, out var value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = new Temperature(t, Units.F);
			DoubleTemperatures.Add(t, value);
			return value;
		}
	}

	private Node0 _data;

	internal Node0 Data
	{
		get
		{
			Node0 node = _data;
			if (node == null)
			{
				Node0 obj = new Node0
				{
					{
						ApiDesignation.D,
						D.Data
					},
					{
						ApiDesignation.E,

	public IEnumerable<ApiValveAndFlange> FindOrifices(ApiDesignation? designation = null, ValveTypeEnum? psvType = null, ApiValveMaterial? valveMaterial = null, Pressure backpressure = null, Temperature reliefTemperature = null, Pressure setPressure = null, RationalNumber? inletSize = null, RationalNumber? outletSize = null, int? inletFlange = null, int? outletFlange = null, bool? allowLightweight = null)
	{
		return _findOrifice(Data.RelevantKeys(designation), psvType, valveMaterial, backpressure, reliefTemperature, setPressure, inletSize, outletSize, inletFlange, outletFlange, allowLightweight);
	}

	public IEnumerable<ApiValveAndFlange> FindOrifices(Area minArea = null, Area maxArea = null, ValveTypeEnum? psvType = null, ApiValveMaterial? valveMaterial = null, Pressure backpressure = null, Temperature reliefTemperature = null, Pressure setPressure = null, RationalNumber? inletSize = null, RationalNumber? outletSize = null, int? inletFlange = null, int? outletFlange = null, bool? allowLightweight = null)
	{
		IEnumerable<ApiDesignation> enumerable;
		if (!(minArea != null))
		{
			IEnumerable<ApiDesignation> keys = ApiValveAndFlange.Areas.Keys;
			enumerable = keys;
		}
		else
		{
			enumerable = from p in ApiValveAndFlange.Areas
				where p.Value >= minArea
				select p.Key;
		}
		IEnumerable<ApiDesignation> enumerable2 = enumerable;
		if (maxArea != null)
		{
			enumerable2 = enumerable2.Where((ApiDesignation d) => ApiValveAndFlange.Areas[d] <= maxArea);
		}
		return _findOrifice(enumerable2, psvType, valveMaterial, backpressure, reliefTemperature, setPressure, inletSize, outletSize, inletFlange, outletFlange, allowLightweight);
	}

	protected IEnumerable<ApiValveAndFlange> _findOrifice(IEnumerable<ApiDesignation> designations, ValveTypeEnum? psvType, ApiValveMaterial? valveMaterial, Pressure backPressure, Temperature reliefTemperature, Pressure setPressure, RationalNumber? inletSize, RationalNumber? outletSize, int? inletFlange, int? outletFlange, bool? allowLightweight)
	{
		if (reliefTemperature != null)
		{
			_ = reliefTemperature > Quantities.GetInF(100);
		}
		List<ApiValveAndFlange> list = new List<ApiValveAndFlange>();
		if (setPressure == null)
		{
			setPressure = Pressure.Absolute(0.0, UnitTypes.Pressure.InnateUnit);
		}
		foreach (ApiDesignation designation in designations)
		{
			foreach (ValveTypeEnum item in Data[designation].RelevantKeys(psvType))
			{
				foreach (ApiValveMaterial item2 in Data[designation][item].RelevantKeys(valveMaterial))
				{
					if (!Data[designation][item].ContainsKey(item2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (ValveAndFlange item3 in from v in Data[designation][item][item2].B3.RelevantKeys(backPressure)
						where v.IsNotExcluded(inletSize, outletSize, inletFlange, outletFlange, allowLightweight)
						select v)
					{
						ApiValveAndFlange apiValveAndFlange = new ApiValveAndFlange(designation, item, item2, item3);
						foreach (TemperatureRange item4 in Data[designation][item][item2].A3.RelevantKeys(reliefTemperature))
						{
							if (Data[designation][item][item2].A3[item4].TryGetValue(item3, out var value) && setPressure <= Quantities.GetInPsig(value.Item1))
							{
								apiValveAndFlange.AddConstraint(new ApiOrificeConstraints(item4.Min, item4.Max, value.Item1, Data[designation][item][item2].B3[item3].Item1, value.Item2, Data[designation][item][item2].B3[item3].Item2));
							}
						}
						if (apiValveAndFlange.Constraints.Count > 0)
						{
							list.Add(apiValveAndFlange);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	public static IEnumerable<ApiSpringMaterial> ApiSpringMaterials(Temperature reliefTemperature)
	{
		if (reliefTemperature < Quantities.GetInF(-450))
		{
			return new ApiSpringMaterial[0];
		}
		if (reliefTemperature < Quantities.GetInF(-75))
		{
			return new ApiSpringMaterial[1];
		}
		if (reliefTemperature <= Quantities.GetInF(450))
		{
			return new ApiSpringMaterial[2]
			{
				ApiSpringMaterial.CarbonSteel,
				ApiSpringMaterial.ChromeAlloySteel
			};
		}
		if (reliefTemperature <= Quantities.GetInF(1000))
		{
			return new ApiSpringMaterial[1] { ApiSpringMaterial.HighTemperatureAlloySteel };
		}
		return new ApiSpringMaterial[0];
	}

	[Obsolete]
	public bool LegacyOrificeDesignate(double setPressure_psig, double designTemperature_F, string designation, double totalBackPressure_psig, ValveTypeEnum valveType, ApiValveMaterial valveMaterial, bool allowLightweight)
	{
		if (!Enum.TryParse<ApiDesignation>(designation, out var result))
		{
			throw new ArgumentException("Cannot parse Designation: " + designation);
		}
		Pressure setPressure = Pressure.Gauge(setPressure_psig, Units.psi);
		Temperature reliefTemperature = new Temperature(designTemperature_F, Units.F);
		Pressure backpressure = Pressure.Gauge(totalBackPressure_psig, Units.psi);
		ApiDesignation? designation2 = result;
		ValveTypeEnum? psvType = valveType;
		ApiValveMaterial? valveMaterial2 = valveMaterial;
		bool? allowLightweight2 = allowLightweight;
		List<ApiValveAndFlange> source = FindOrifices(designation2, psvType, valveMaterial2, backpressure, reliefTemperature, setPressure, null, null, null, null, allowLightweight2).ToList();
		if (!source.Any())
		{
			return false;
		}
		ApiValveAndFlange apiValveAndFlange = source.First();
		LegacyValveInSize = apiValveAndFlange.InletSize;
		LegacyValveOutSize = apiValveAndFlange.OutletSize;
		LegacyValveInRating = apiValveAndFlange.InletFlange;
		LegacyValveOutRating = apiValveAndFlange.OutletFlange;
		return true;
	}
}

			RationalNumber? outletCenterToFace = 6;
			isLightWeight = false;
			Sl3 = new ValveAndFlange(sl2, null, null, null, null, null, outletCenterToFace, isLightWeight);
			ValveAndFlange sl3 = Sl3;
			inletFlange = 600;
			Sl4 = new ValveAndFlange(sl3, null, null, inletFlange);
			ValveAndFlange sl4 = Sl4;
			outletCenterToFace = 3;
			inletFlange = 900;
			int? outletFlange = 300;
			RationalNumber? outletCenterToFace2 = RationalNumber.MixedNumber(6, 1u, 2);
			Sl5 = new ValveAndFlange(sl4, null, outletCenterToFace, inletFlange, outletFlange, null, outletCenterToFace2);
			ValveAndFlange sl5 = Sl5;
			outletFlange = 1500;
			Sl6 = new ValveAndFlange(sl5, null, null, outletFlange);
			ValveAndFlange sl6 = Sl6;
			outletFlange = 2500;
			outletCenterToFace2 = RationalNumber.MixedNumber(5, 1u, 2);
			outletCenterToFace = 7;
			Sl7 = new ValveAndFlange(sl6, null, null, outletFlange, null, outletCenterToFace2, outletCenterToFace);
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper] = ((ApiTables5thEd)_parent)._F.PilotOperated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper];
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20] = ((ApiTables5thEd)_parent)._F.PilotOperated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20];
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				NodeA3 a = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel].A3;
				NodeA4 nodeA = a[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)];
				nodeA[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3650, item2: true);
				nodeA[Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3650, item2: true);
				a[new TemperatureRange(101, 500)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3650, item2: true);
				NodeA3 a2 = PilotOperated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3;
				NodeA4 nodeA2 = a2[new TemperatureRange(-450, -21)];
				nodeA2[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3550, item2: true);
				nodeA2[Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3550, item2: true);
				NodeA4 nodeA3 = a2[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)];
				nodeA3[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3550, item2: true);
				nodeA3[Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3550, item2: true);
				a2[new TemperatureRange(101, 500)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3550, item2: true);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				NodeA3 a = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel].A3;
				a[new TemperatureRange(101, 500)][Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2900, item2: true);
				TemperatureRange key = new TemperatureRange(-20, 100);
				NodeA4 nodeA = a[key];
				nodeA[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1240, item2: true);
				nodeA[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2900, item2: true);
				NodeA3 a2 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3;
				NodeA4 nodeA2 = a2[key];
				nodeA2[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1200, item2: true);
				nodeA2[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2825, item2: true);
				NodeA4 nodeA3 = a2[new TemperatureRange(-450, -21)];
				nodeA3[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1200, item2: true);
				nodeA3[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2825, item2: true);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				Node3 node = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel];
				node.B3[Po5B] = Tuple.Create(1480, item2: true);
				NodeA4 nodeA = node.A3[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)];
				nodeA[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1305, item2: true);
				nodeA[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3080, item2: true);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].B3[Po5B] = Tuple.Create(1440, item2: true);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper].B3[Po5B] = Tuple.Create(1440, item2: true);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20].B3[Po5B] = Tuple.Create(1440, item2: true);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].Remove(Po3S);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].Remove(Po5S);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper].Remove(Po3B);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

			RationalNumber? outletCenterToFace = 10;
			isLightWeight = false;

			ValveAndFlange sl3 = Sl3;
			inletFlange = 600;

			ValveAndFlange sl4 = Sl4;

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				NodeA3 a = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel].A3;
				a[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(4620, item2: false);
				a[new TemperatureRange(101, 500)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(4620, item2: false);
				NodeA3 a2 = PilotOperated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3;
				a2[new TemperatureRange(-450, -21)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(4495, item2: false);
				a2[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(4495, item2: false);
				NodeA3 a3 = PilotOperated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20].A3;
				a3[new TemperatureRange(-20, 100)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3750, item2: false);
				a3[new TemperatureRange(101, 300)][Po6S] = Tuple.Create(3750, item2: false);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				NodeA4 nodeA = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3[new TemperatureRange(-450, -21)];
				nodeA[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1375, item2: false);
				nodeA[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3530, item2: false);
				TemperatureRange key = new TemperatureRange(-20, 100);
				NodeA4 nodeA2 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel].A3[key];
				nodeA2[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1425, item2: false);
				nodeA2[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3630, item2: false);
				NodeA4 nodeA3 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3[key];
				nodeA3[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1375, item2: false);
				nodeA3[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3530, item2: false);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper].A3[key][Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1150, item2: false);
				NodeA4 nodeA4 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20].A3[key];
				nodeA4[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1150, item2: false);
				nodeA4[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2940, item2: false);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		internal override Node2 PilotOperated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_updatedPilotOperated)
				{
					return base.PilotOperated;
				}
				_updatedPilotOperated = true;
				NodeA4 nodeA = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3[new TemperatureRange(-450, -21)];
				nodeA[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1375, item2: false);
				nodeA[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3520, item2: false);
				TemperatureRange key = new TemperatureRange(-20, 100);
				NodeA4 nodeA2 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.CarbonSteel].A3[key];
				nodeA2[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1425, item2: false);
				nodeA2[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3630, item2: false);
				NodeA4 nodeA3 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.AusteniticStainless].A3[key];
				nodeA3[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1375, item2: false);
				nodeA3[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(3520, item2: false);
				NodeA4 nodeA4 = base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.Alloy20].A3[key];
				nodeA4[Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1150, item2: false);
				nodeA4[Po5S] = Tuple.Create(2940, item2: false);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper].Remove(Po3B);
				base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated[ApiValveMaterial.NickelCopper].A3[key][Po3S] = Tuple.Create(1150, item2: false);
				return base.PilotOperatedEncapsulated;
			}
		}

		public PBuilder(ApiTables parent)
			: base(parent, isNiCuPilotVafListRestricted: true)

		static PBuilder()

			ValveAndFlange sl2 = Sl2;

			RationalNumber? outletCenterToFace = 10;
			isLightWeight = false;
			Sl3 = new ValveAndFlange(sl2, null, null, null, null, inletCenterToFace, outletCenterToFace, isLightWeight);
			ValveAndFlange sl3 = Sl3;

public sealed class ApiValveAndFlange
{
	public readonly ApiDesignation Designation;

	public readonly ValveTypeEnum PsvType;

	public readonly ApiValveMaterial ValveMaterial;

	public readonly RationalNumber InletSize;

	public readonly RationalNumber OutletSize;

	public readonly int InletFlange;

	public readonly int OutletFlange;

	public readonly RationalNumber InletCenterToFace;

	public readonly RationalNumber OutletCenterToFace;

	internal readonly ApiTables.ValveAndFlange ValveAndFlange;

	public readonly bool IsLightWeight;

	private readonly List<ApiOrificeConstraints> _constraints;

	public static Dictionary<ApiDesignation, Area> Areas = new Dictionary<ApiDesignation, Area>
	{
		{
			ApiDesignation.D,
			new Area(0.11, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.E,
			new Area(0.196, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.F,
			new Area(0.307, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.G,
			new Area(0.503, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.H,
			new Area(0.785, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.J,
			new Area(1.287, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.K,
			new Area(1.838, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.L,
			new Area(2.853, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.M,
			new Area(3.6, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.N,
			new Area(4.34, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.P,
			new Area(6.38, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.Q,
			new Area(11.05, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.R,
			new Area(16.0, Units.in2)
		},
		{
			ApiDesignation.T,
			new Area(26.0, Units.in2)
		}
	};

	public Area Area => Areas[Designation];

	public IReadOnlyList<ApiOrificeConstraints> Constraints => _constraints;

	internal ApiValveAndFlange(ApiDesignation designation, ValveTypeEnum psvType, ApiValveMaterial valveMaterial, ApiTables.ValveAndFlange vaf)
	{
		Designation = designation;
		PsvType = psvType;
		ValveMaterial = valveMaterial;
		InletSize = vaf.InletSize;
		OutletSize = vaf.OutletSize;
		InletFlange = vaf.InletFlange;
		OutletFlange = vaf.OutletFlange;
		InletCenterToFace = vaf.InletCenterToFace;
		OutletCenterToFace = vaf.OutletCenterToFace;
		ValveAndFlange = vaf;
		IsLightWeight = vaf.IsLightWeight;
		_constraints = new List<ApiOrificeConstraints>();
	}

	internal void AddConstraint(ApiOrificeConstraints constraint)
	{
		_constraints.Add(constraint);
	}

	public Pressure MaxSetPressure(Temperature t)
	{
		if (!Constraints.Any((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature > t))
		{
			return new Pressure();
		}
		return Constraints.Single((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature >= t).MaxSetPressure;
	}

	public Pressure OutletPressureLimit(Temperature t)
	{
		if (!Constraints.Any((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature > t))
		{
			return new Pressure();
		}
		return Constraints.Single((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature > t).OutletPressureLimit;
	}

	public Temperature MinTemperature()
	{
		return Constraints.Select((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature).Min();
	}

	public Temperature MaxTemperature()
	{
		return Constraints.Select((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MaxTemperature).Max();
	}

	public bool? IsMaxSetPressureASMEFlangeLimit(Temperature t)
	{
		if (!Constraints.Any((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature >= t))
		{
			return null;
		}
		return Constraints.Single((ApiOrificeConstraints c) => c.MinTemperature <= t && c.MaxTemperature >= t).IsSetPressureLimitASMEFlangeLimit;
	}

	public override bool Equals(object obj)
	{
		if (!(obj is ApiValveAndFlange))
		{
			return false;
		}
		ApiValveAndFlange apiValveAndFlange = (ApiValveAndFlange)obj;
		if (Designation == apiValveAndFlange.Designation && PsvType == apiValveAndFlange.PsvType && ValveMaterial == apiValveAndFlange.ValveMaterial && InletSize == apiValveAndFlange.InletSize && OutletSize == apiValveAndFlange.OutletSize && InletFlange == apiValveAndFlange.InletFlange && OutletFlange == apiValveAndFlange.OutletFlange && InletCenterToFace == apiValveAndFlange.InletCenterToFace && OutletCenterToFace == apiValveAndFlange.OutletCenterToFace)
		{
			return IsLightWeight == apiValveAndFlange.IsLightWeight;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool operator ==(ApiValveAndFlange lhs, ApiValveAndFlange rhs)
	{
		return lhs.Equals(rhs);
	}

	public static bool operator !=(ApiValveAndFlange lhs, ApiValveAndFlange rhs)
	{
		return !(lhs == rhs);
	}

	public override int GetHashCode()
	{
		return Designation.GetHashCode() * 11 + ValveAndFlange.GetHashCode() * 13;
	}
}
